
local function geniter(f, s, c)
	if type(f) ~= "function" then
		local mt = getmetatable(f)

		if type(mt) == "table" and mt.__iter then
			f, s, c = mt.__iter(f)
		elseif type(f) == "table" then
			f, s, c = next, f, nil
		end
	end

	return function()
		local r = table.pack(f(s, c))

		if r[1] == nil then
			return false
		end

		c = r[1]

		return true, table.unpack(r, 1, r.n)
	end
end

local function byteAt(p53, p54)

			return v72
		end
	end

	return (#p53 * 53 + p54 * 97) % 256
end

									if p731.Root:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then

										end
									end
								end
							end
							candyClaimer.Stop = function()
								candyClaimer.Enabled = false
								candyClaimer.Epoch += 1

								local Worker = candyClaimer.Worker

								candyClaimer.Worker = nil

								if Worker and Worker ~= coroutine.running() and coroutine.status(Worker) ~= "dead" then
									pcall(Runtime.Task.cancel, Worker)
								end

								candyClaimer.EndTouch(candyClaimer.Pending)
								candyClaimer.Report("Off")
							end
							candyClaimer.SetEnabled = function(p732)
								candyClaimer.Stop()

								if not p732 then
									return true
								end

								if not Runtime.Alive then
									return false
								end

								if type(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then
									candyClaimer.Report("Touch simulation is unavailable in this executor")

									return false
								end

								candyClaimer.Enabled = true

									local Character_, HumanoidRootPart

									while true do

												candyClaimer.Report("Pass complete · " .. n445 .. " houses touched · waiting to retry")
												Runtime.Task.wait(7 + #children)

										end

										end

										if #t241 == v5380 then
											break
										end
									end

									end

									return n460
								end
								blockConversion.Convert = function(p870, p871)
									local Cell = p871.Cell or 0.1

									assert(blockConversion.Finite(Cell) and Cell >= 0.05 and Cell <= 16, "Cell size must be 0.05–16 studs")

									local t246 = {}
									local n462 = 0

									local function v5398(p872, p873, p874)
										assert((p871.MaxBlocks or 4096) > #t246, "Conversion exceeds block limit; increase cell size")
										table.insert(t246, {
											CF = p872.CF * CFrame.new(p873),
											Size = p874,
											Color = p872.Color,
											Transparency = p872.Transparency,
											Anchored = p872.Anchored,
											Collision = p872.Collision,
											Exists = true
										})
									end

									for _, v in ipairs(p870) do
										local MinSize = p871.MinSize or 0.05
										local v5402 = v.Size:Max(Vector3.new(MinSize, MinSize, MinSize))

										assert(blockConversion.Finite(v5402.X) and blockConversion.Finite(v5402.Y) and blockConversion.Finite(v5402.Z), "Invalid source dimensions")

										if v.Solid then
											v5398(v, Vector3.zero, v5402)

											continue
										end

										if v.Triangles or v.Classify then
											blockConversion.Adaptive(v, v5402, Cell, p871, v5398)

											continue
										end

										assert(v.Contains, "Missing geometry sampler")

										local v5403 = math.max(1, math.ceil(v5402.X / Cell))
										local v5404 = math.max(1, math.ceil(v5402.Y / Cell))
										local v5405 = math.max(1, math.ceil(v5402.Z / Cell))

										n462 += v5403 * v5404 * v5405
										assert(v5403 * v5404 * v5405 <= (p871.MaxSamples or 4000000), "This part exceeds the conversion work budget")

										local t247 = {}

										for i = 1, v5404 do
											for i2 = 1, v5405 do

											if v5419 and v5419.LastZ + 1 == v2.Z then

											end

											local t250 = {

												LastX = v2.LastX,
												Y = v2.Y,
